## Choose Himyarite Honorific
ME_choose_honorific_himyarite = {
	picture = {
		reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/decisions/decision_legitimacy.dds"
	}
	sort_order = 40

	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			culture = { has_cultural_pillar = language_sayhadic }
			dynasty = {
			 	has_dynasty_modifier = himyarite_heritage_dynasty_modifier
			}
		}
		has_any_nickname = no
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "decision_view_widget_option_list_generic"
		controller = decision_option_list_controller
		decision_to_second_step_button = "CHOOSE_HONORIFIC"
		show_from_start = yes

		item = { # Yahn'am
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahn_am
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahn_am_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahn_am
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yaha'man
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yaha_man
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yaha_man_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yaha_man
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yahqabid
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahqabid
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahqabid_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahqabid
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yahasdaq
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahasdaq
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahasdaq_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahasdaq
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yahbur
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahbur
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahbur_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahbur
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yahahmad
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahahmad
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahahmad_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahahmad
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yaharhib
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yaharhib
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yaharhib_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yaharhib
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Yahar_ish
			value = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahar_ish
			current_description = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahar_ish_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_himyarite_yahar_ish
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
	}

	effect = {
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yahn_am = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yahn_am
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yaha_man = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yaha_man
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yahqabid = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yahqabid
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yahasdaq = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yahasdaq
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yahbur = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yahbur
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yahahmad = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yahahmad
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yaharhib = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yaharhib
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_himyarite_yahar_ish = {
				give_nickname = nick_me_yahar_ish
			}
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 3 #Only Kings and Emperors
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 100
	}
}

## Choose Caliphal Honorific - Main
ME_choose_honorific_caliphal_main = {
	picture = {
		reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/decisions/decision_abbasid_palace.dds"
	}
	sort_order = 40

	is_shown = {
		faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
		any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
		OR = {
			has_any_nickname = no
			AND = {
				exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
				var:nicknamed_target_var = { faith.religion = religion:islam_religion }
				var:nicknamed_target_var = { has_any_nickname = no }
				var:nicknamed_target_var = { is_close_or_extended_family_of = root }
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		is_ai = no
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "decision_view_widget_option_list_generic"
		controller = decision_option_list_controller
		decision_to_second_step_button = "CHOOSE_LAQAB_ENDING"
		show_from_start = yes

		item = { # Short Nicknames
			value = choose_honorific_caliphal_short
			current_description = choose_honorific_caliphal_short_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_caliphal_short
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Long Nicknames
			value = choose_honorific_caliphal_long
			current_description = choose_honorific_caliphal_long_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_caliphal_long
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		
		item = { # Take the Laqab yourself
			value = choose_honorific_caliphal_personal
			current_description = choose_honorific_islamic_personal_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_islamic_personal
			#icon = "gfx/interface/icons/widgets/icon_group_of_characters.dds"
			is_shown = {
				has_any_nickname = no
				exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
				NOT = { var:nicknamed_target_var = root }
			}
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
	}

	effect = {
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:choose_honorific_caliphal_short = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_caliphal_short_tt
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_caliphal_short
					player = root
				}
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_caliphal_long = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_caliphal_long_tt
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_caliphal_long
					player = root
				}
			}
			
			scope:choose_honorific_caliphal_personal = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_islamic_personal_tt
				remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_caliphal_main
					player = root
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var }
					NOT = { var:nicknamed_target_var = { faith.religion = religion:islam_religion } }
					NOT = { var:nicknamed_target_var = { is_close_or_extended_family_of = root } }
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = nicknamed_target_var
				value = root
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustazhir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_mustakfi = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustakfi_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_mustamsik = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustamsik_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_mustanjid = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustanjid_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_mustansir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustansir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_mustarshid = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustarshid_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_mustaqwi = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_mustaqwi_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_muwaffaq = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_muwaffaq_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_nasir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_nasir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_qadir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_qadir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_qahir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_qahir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_qaim = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_qaim_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_radi = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_radi_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_rashid = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_rashid_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_rashI_d = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_rashI_d_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_sami = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_sami_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_shakir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_shakir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_tai = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_tai_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_wathiq = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_wathiq_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_zafir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_zafir_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_zahir = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_zahir_nonprefix }
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:nicknamed_target = root
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:nicknamed_target = root }
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = -50
			scope:nicknamed_target = {
				add_prestige = 75
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = me_bestowed_laqab_opinion
					target = root
					opinion = 10
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = { # Return to main decision
			limit = { scope:ME_honorific_return_option = yes }
			
			scope:nicknamed_target = { save_scope_as = target }
			custom_tooltip = ME_honorific_return_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
				player = root
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_shakir_lillah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_shakir_lillah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_tai_lillah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_tai_lillah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_tai_li_amr_allah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_tai_li_amr_allah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_wathiq_billah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_wathiq_billah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_zahir_bi_amr_allah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_zahir_bi_amr_allah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_zahir_li_izaz_din_allah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_zahir_li_izaz_din_allah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_zafir_bi_ada_allah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_zafir_bi_ada_allah_nonprefix }
				}
				scope:choose_honorific_al_zafir_bi_amr_allah = { #
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_me_al_zafir_bi_amr_allah_nonprefix }
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:nicknamed_target = root
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:nicknamed_target = root }
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = -50
			scope:nicknamed_target = {
				add_prestige = 75
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = me_bestowed_laqab_opinion
					target = root
					opinion = 10
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = { # Return to main decision
			limit = { scope:ME_honorific_return_option = yes }
			
			scope:nicknamed_target = { save_scope_as = target }
			custom_tooltip = ME_honorific_return_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
				player = root
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no 
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

## Choose Islamic Honorific - Main
ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main = {
	picture = {
		reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/decisions/decision_muslim_leader.dds"
	}
	sort_order = 40

	is_shown = {
		faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
		current_date >= 950.1.1
		OR = {
			has_any_nickname = no
			AND = {
				exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
				var:nicknamed_target_var = { has_any_nickname = no }
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		is_ai = no
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "decision_view_widget_option_list_generic"
		controller = decision_option_list_controller
		decision_to_second_step_button = "CHOOSE_LAQAB_ENDING"
		show_from_start = yes

		item = { # Ends in ad-Din
			value = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din
			current_description = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Ends in al-Islam
			value = choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam
			current_description = choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam
			is_valid = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var }
					AND = {
						exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
						var:nicknamed_target_var.faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
					}
				}
			}
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Ends in ad-Dawlah
			value = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah
			current_description = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		item = { # Ends in al-Mulk
			value = choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk
			current_description = choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
		
		item = { # Take the Laqab yourself
			value = choose_honorific_islamic_personal
			current_description = choose_honorific_islamic_personal_desc
			localization = choose_honorific_islamic_personal
			#icon = "gfx/interface/icons/widgets/icon_group_of_characters.dds"
			is_shown = {
				has_any_nickname = no
				exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
				NOT = { var:nicknamed_target_var = root }
			}
			ai_chance = { value = 10 }
		}
	}

	effect = {
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din_tt
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din
					player = root
				}
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam_tt
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam
					player = root
				}
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah_tt
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah
					player = root
				}
			}
			scope:choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk_tt
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk
					player = root
				}
			}
			
			scope:choose_honorific_islamic_personal = {

				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_islamic_personal_tt
				remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
				open_view_data = {
					view = decision_detail
					data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
					player = root
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var } }
			set_variable = {
				name = nicknamed_target_var
				value = root
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var }
			var:nicknamed_target_var = { save_scope_as = nicknamed_target }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var } }
			save_scope_as = nicknamed_target
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes }
			
			remove_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_prefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din
				player = root
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes }
			
			set_variable = {
				name = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
				value = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_ad_din
				player = root
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = no
				scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var } }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_prefixed_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_nonprefixed_tt
			}

					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}

		}

			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:nicknamed_target = root }
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = -50
			scope:nicknamed_target = {
				add_prestige = 75
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = me_bestowed_laqab_opinion
					target = root
					opinion = 10
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = { # Return to main decision
			limit = { scope:ME_honorific_return_option = yes }
			
			scope:nicknamed_target = { save_scope_as = target }
			custom_tooltip = ME_honorific_return_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
				player = root
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no 
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

## Choose Islamic Honorific - al-Islam

	picture = {
		reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/decisions/decision_muslim_leader.dds"
	}
	is_invisible = yes

	is_shown = {
		faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
		current_date >= 950.1.1
		exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		var:nicknamed_target_var = {
			has_any_nickname = no
			faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
		}
		is_ai = no
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "decision_view_widget_option_list_generic"

		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var }
			var:nicknamed_target_var = { save_scope_as = nicknamed_target }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var } }
			save_scope_as = nicknamed_target
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes }
			
			remove_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_prefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam
				player = root
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes }
			
			set_variable = {
				name = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
				value = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_al_islam
				player = root
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = no
				scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var } }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_prefixed_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_nonprefixed_tt
			}

				}
			}
		}

			limit = {
				scope:nicknamed_target = root
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = 50
			add_character_flag = has_islam_only_laqab
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:nicknamed_target = root }
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = -50
			scope:nicknamed_target = {
				add_prestige = 75
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = me_bestowed_laqab_opinion
					target = root
					opinion = 10
				}
				add_character_flag = has_islam_only_laqab
			}
		}
		
		if = { # Return to main decision
			limit = { scope:ME_honorific_return_option = yes }
			
			scope:nicknamed_target = { save_scope_as = target }
			custom_tooltip = ME_honorific_return_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
				player = root
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no 
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

## Choose Islamic Honorific - ad-Dawlah

	picture = {
		reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/decisions/decision_muslim_leader.dds"
	}
	is_invisible = yes

	is_shown = {
		faith.religion = religion:islam_religion
		current_date >= 950.1.1
		exists = var:nicknamed_target_var
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		var:nicknamed_target_var = { has_any_nickname = no }
		is_ai = no
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "decision_view_widget_option_list_generic"

		item = { # 
			value = choose_honorific_become_prefixed

		}
		item = { # 
			value = choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed
			current_description = choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed_desc

	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var }
			var:nicknamed_target_var = { save_scope_as = nicknamed_target }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var } }
			save_scope_as = nicknamed_target
		}
			
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes }
			
			remove_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_prefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah
				player = root
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes }
			
			set_variable = {
				name = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
				value = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_ad_dawlah
				player = root
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = no
				scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var } }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_prefixed_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_nonprefixed_tt
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var } }
			switch = {
				trigger = yes 
				scope:choose_honorific_adud_ud_dawlah = { # Aḍud ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_adud_ud_dawlah }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_ala_ud_dawlah = { # Ala' ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_ala_ud_dawlah }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_alam_ud_dawlah = { # Alam ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_alam_ud_dawlah }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_alim_ud_dawlah = { # Alim ud-Dawlah

				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_sultanat_ud_dawlah = { # Sultanat ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_sultanat_ud_dawlah_nonprefix }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_waliyat_ud_dawlah = { # Waliyat ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_waliyat_ud_dawlah_nonprefix }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_zahirat_ud_dawlah = { # Zahirat ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_zahirat_ud_dawlah_nonprefix }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_zahrat_ud_dawlah = { # Zahrat ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_zahrat_ud_dawlah_nonprefix }
				} 
				scope:choose_honorific_zaimat_ud_dawlah = { # Zaimat ud-Dawlah
					scope:nicknamed_target = { give_nickname = nick_zaimat_ud_dawlah_nonprefix }
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:nicknamed_target = root
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:nicknamed_target = root }
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			add_prestige = -50
			scope:nicknamed_target = {
				add_prestige = 75
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = me_bestowed_laqab_opinion
					target = root
					opinion = 10
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = { # Return to main decision
			limit = { scope:ME_honorific_return_option = yes }
			
			scope:nicknamed_target = { save_scope_as = target }
			custom_tooltip = ME_honorific_return_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
				player = root
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no 
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
		}
	}
	ai_check_interval = 60
	ai_potential = {
		highest_held_title_tier > 2
		is_independent_ruler = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0 # Disabled for now
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var }
			var:nicknamed_target_var = { save_scope_as = nicknamed_target }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:nicknamed_target_var } }
			save_scope_as = nicknamed_target
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes }
			
			remove_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_prefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk
				player = root
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes }
			
			set_variable = {
				name = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var
				value = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_al_mulk
				player = root
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = no
				scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var } }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_prefixed_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_variable = choose_honorific_nonprefixed_var }
				custom_tooltip = choose_honorific_nick_nonprefixed_tt
			}

					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes

			add_prestige = -50
			scope:nicknamed_target = {
				add_prestige = 75
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = me_bestowed_laqab_opinion
					target = root
					opinion = 10
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = { # Return to main decision
			limit = { scope:ME_honorific_return_option = yes }
			
			scope:nicknamed_target = { save_scope_as = target }
			custom_tooltip = ME_honorific_return_tt
			open_view_data = {
				view = decision_detail
				data = decision:ME_choose_honorific_islamic_main
				player = root
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ME_honorific_return_option = no 
				NOR = {
					scope:choose_honorific_become_nonprefixed = yes
					scope:choose_honorific_become_prefixed = yes
				}
			}
			remove_variable = nicknamed_target_var
		}
